# Nathan Direct: Subject Router (2023–2024 + Reviewed Highlights)

**Coverage labels are essential.** This is a **working topical front door** to the 802 raw user messages currently captured from 2023–2024, the 143 dated entries in the verified-words compendium, and the 10-item theorist feed. It is **not** a complete subject encyclopedia across 20,575 messages or three repositories. Every link preserves primary source identity. `MSG` tags come from the individual user message; compendium tags are reviewed/documented near excerpts; feed tags denote downstream subject routing. We do not silently merge unlike taxonomies.

**Also see:** [White-glove master locator](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/WORKSPACES/COMMON/NATHAN_DIRECT_WHITE_GLOVE_LOCATOR_INDEX_2026-10-24.md) (all 146 ledgers, 143 compendium extracts, 10 feed items and source controls); [early full 802-message locator](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/WORKSPACES/COMMON/NATHAN_DIRECT_2023_2024_MESSAGE_TOPIC_LOCATOR_2026-10-24.md) (all user messages including messages not assigned a topic).

## Uncovered source classes

This topic index omits original SAT plain-text transcripts not normalized into HsH raw JSON, podcast transcripts, diagrams/PDFs, and extensive later 2025–2026 raw user messages. `UNLABELED-DIRECT-TOPIC` is **not an absence-of-ideas judgment**: it means this version of the machine tagger did not assign a direct MSG topic.
